# EFTA Investigation — Network Registry

## Evidence-Tiered Roster of Every Person Identified

**Version 1.0** · Compiled 31 July 2026 · **317 persons**

*Companion to: Entity Database v2.2, Master Document v10.0, Network Hierarchy v1.0*

*Sources: DOJ EFTA releases, Giuffre v. Maxwell record, 2007 NPA/CVRA litigation, FBI 302s, SDNY prosecution memoranda, House Oversight materials, and web verification current to 31 July 2026.*

> **READ THIS BEFORE READING ANYTHING ELSE.**
>
> Presence in this registry does **not** establish criminal conduct. Tier reflects the strength and
> nature of *documented evidence*, not guilt. The overwhelming majority of people named here have
> **never been charged with any crime**, and each entry says so explicitly where true.
>
> **Operators** (people who ran the operation) and **client-abusers** (people alleged to have abused
> victims) are tracked as independent attributes and are never merged into a single list. Some people
> are both; where so, the entry says so.
>
> **Tier 1J is not Tier 1.** It marks people named as abusers in the DS12 victim journals who have
> never been charged. On **25 April 2026**, Judge Jessica Clarke (S.D.N.Y., *Doe v. Black*) found the
> plaintiff had **falsified sonogram images** in those journals, sanctioned Wigdor LLP and Jeanne
> Christensen for lying to the court and directing destruction of evidence, and **barred the falsified
> entries from trial** — while **denying** case-terminating sanctions and allowing the underlying rape
> claim to proceed. The journals are therefore *partially* discredited, not wholly. Tier 1J exists so
> that distinction is impossible to lose.

#### Ghislaine Maxwell  `T1`

**Branch:** principal · **Roles:** recruiter, groomer, trafficker, coconspirator, planner, scheduler, operations  
**OPERATOR** · **CLIENT / ALLEGED ABUSER**

**Tier basis:** Convicted December 2021 in SDNY on five counts including sex trafficking of a minor; sentenced to 20 years — the only person convicted of an Epstein-related sex offense.

**Knowledge:** organizer — The SDNY prosecution memo describes her as 'co-conspirator, recruiter, participant throughout'; the FBI organizational hierarchy places her directly under Epstein for recruitment, grooming and property management; four victims testified against her at trial.

**Criminal status:** convicted: Dec 2021 (SDNY), sex trafficking of a minor and related counts; sentenced June 2022 to 20 years; Supreme Court declined review Oct 2025; incarcerated at FPC Bryan, a minimum-security federal prison camp, Bryan, Texas, after an unexplained 2025 transfer from FCI Tallahassee

**Civil:** Settled Giuffre v. Maxwell (2017, confidential); subject of ongoing civil discovery disputes

**Fallout:** N/A — incarcerated

**Status, July 2026:** Federal inmate, FPC Bryan, as of July 2026. Commutation application pending with the Trump administration; no commutation granted as of 2026-07-31. Whistleblower allegations of preferential treatment triggered a Rep. Raskin probe; Sen. Whitehouse is demanding DOJ documents on the transfer; Raskin filed a House resolution opposing any pardon and a Senate resolution was reported July 29, 2026.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Directed a victim to engage in sex acts with Glenn Dubin — `EFTA02731082, p.57`

- *[alleged]* Told a victim 'she was going to meet a prince and should make him happy and do the exact same things for him that she did for Epstein' — `EFTA02731082, p.57`

- *[alleged]* Kept binders of nude and partially nude photographs — `EFTA02731082, p.66`

- *[court-found]* Sex trafficking of a minor, transporting a minor with intent to engage in criminal sexual activity, and related conspiracy counts — `USA v. Maxwell, 20 Cr. 330 (SDNY)`

- *[alleged]* Directed a victim to give erotic massages and engage in sexual activity with Jean-Luc Brunel — `EFTA00263917, Giuffre testimony`

- *[alleged]* Trafficked the minor Leon Black victim, per the Manhattan DA's briefing to SDNY — `EFTA02731593; EFTA02731636`


**Their response:** Maintains her innocence. Her legal team has consistently argued she was prosecuted for Epstein's crimes. She declined to answer questions in the 2026 congressional context while pursuing clemency.


**Timeline**


- **2019-07** — Named one of four subjects for 'immediate approach' in the SDNY arrest briefing (EFTA01653331)

- **2019-12-19** — SDNY prosecution memo anticipates recommending transportation-of-minors charges against her (EFTA02731082 fn.62)

- **2020-07-02** — Arrested

- **2021-12-29** — Convicted on 5 of 6 counts

- **2022-06** — Sentenced to 20 years

- **2025-10** — U.S. Supreme Court declines to hear her appeal, exhausting direct review

- **2026-02-10** — NPR reports she formally appealed for clemency while declining to answer questions

- **2026-07-29** — Senate resolution opposing a pardon reported (Washington Post)

#### Jean-Luc Brunel  `T1`

**Branch:** recruitment · **Roles:** recruiter, trafficker, groomer, operations, coconspirator, client  
**OPERATOR** · **CLIENT / ALLEGED ABUSER**

**Tier basis:** Arrested December 2020 at Paris CDG and formally charged in France with rape of a minor over 15 and criminal conspiracy to commit sexual assault; died in pre-trial detention February 2022 without trial or conviction.

**Knowledge:** organizer — His business partner Sergio Cordero told the FBI in 2010 that MC2's 'principal business... is to transport underage girls for purposes of sexual pleasure for Brunel and Epstein' and that Brunel 'has always been Epstein's principal supplier of underage girls using MC2 as a cover up.'

**Criminal status:** charged: arrested Dec 2020 in France and charged with rape of a minor over 15 and criminal conspiracy to commit sexual assault; died in pre-trial detention 2022-02-19 — never tried, never convicted. Never charged by U.S. authorities.

**Civil:** Named in Giuffre and other victim litigation; MC2 was central to the recruitment pipeline

**Fallout:** MC2 Model Management collapsed

**Status, July 2026:** Deceased (2022-02-19, age 76). His case is not closed: French prosecutors reopened their probe into Epstein associates including Brunel on 2026-02-20.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Used MC2 Model Management as a front to transport underage girls into the United States for sex — `EFTA01657299, FBI internal email, 2010-06-10 (Sergio Cordero interview)`

- *[alleged]* 'Jean Luc Brunel, owner of MC2 Model Management and Jeffrey Epstein engaged in racketeering that involved luring in minor children for sexual play for money' — `EFTA02810334 pp.60-62, JPMorgan Due Diligence Review, April 2011`

- *[alleged]* Nearly two dozen models said they had been sexually assaulted by Brunel — `CBS 60 Minutes / Diane Sawyer investigation, 1988, reported in EFTA01480954`

- *[alleged]* French victim testimonies describing models drugged and raped at his home from the 1980s onward — `EFTA00079597, pp.4-5`

- *[alleged]* Controlled a victim's U.S. visa and, with Epstein, refused to renew it in 2011 — `EFTA02731082, pp.47-48`

- *[alleged]* Brought a 15-year-old to Epstein's island — `EFTA02731082, pp.46-48`

- *[alleged]* 'Brunel has been reported to have recruited minors for sexual activity with Epstein, and to have himself participated in sexual activity with minors' — `EFTA01653331, SDNY arrest briefing, 2019-07-03`

- *[court-found]* French charges: rape of a minor under 15, rape of a minor over 15, sexual assault, and participation in an association of criminals — `EFTA00079597; French investigation, Paris`


**Their response:** Denied all allegations before his death. His lawyers stated his death 'was not guided by guilt but a profound sense of injustice.'


**Timeline**

- **1988** — CBS 60 Minutes / Diane Sawyer exposé — nearly two dozen models allege sexual assault (EFTA01480954)

- **2004-09** — Epstein wires $1M; MC2 founded with Brunel as 85% owner (EFTA01480954)

#### Jeffrey Epstein  `T1`

*Also: Jeffrey E. Epstein, Jeffrey Edward Epstein*

**Branch:** principal · **Roles:** financier, trafficker, groomer, recruiter, planner, admin, coconspirator, client  
**OPERATOR** · **CLIENT / ALLEGED ABUSER**

**Tier basis:** Pled guilty to Florida state sex offenses in 2008 and was federally charged by SDNY in July 2019 with sex trafficking of minors and conspiracy; died in custody before trial.

**Knowledge:** organizer — He designed and directed the operation: he personally instructed victims to give 'massages' to named men, directed destruction of contact books and computers during the 2005 Florida investigation, warned departing staff to 'keep your mouth shut', diverted an aircraft to defeat service of an FBI target letter, and built trust instruments containing employment-cliff and 'disloyalty' forfeiture clauses SDNY treated as potential witness intimidation.

**Criminal status:** convicted: 2008 Florida state plea (solicitation of prostitution / procuring a minor for prostitution), served 13 of 18 months; charged: July 2019 SDNY indictment for sex trafficking of minors and conspiracy, 19 Cr. 490 (RMB) — died in custody before trial, so never tried on the federal charges

**Civil:** 17+ out-of-court civil settlements documented pre-2011; estate settled multiple victim suits and funded the Epstein Victims' Compensation Program; estate valued at $577,672,654 at death

**Fallout:** Died in custody 2019-08-10; estate wound down through co-executors Darren Indyke and Richard Kahn

**Status, July 2026:** Deceased. Estate administration and the Epstein Victims' Compensation Program continue; his files are the subject of the Epstein Files Transparency Act releases and the June-July 2026 Judge Emmet Sullivan unredaction litigation.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Directed a victim to massage Jes Staley at his New York residence; after she reported being raped, told her 'he left it to [her] and Staley to decide whether to engage in sex' — `EFTA02731082, SDNY prosecution memo, p.32`

- *[alleged]* Directed the same victim to massage Leon Black; when she complained of Black's sexual contact, 'laughed it off as a funny incident' — `EFTA02731082, p.32`

- *[alleged]* Directed destruction of contact books and computers from Palm Beach and shredding of Virgin Islands directories during the Florida investigation — `EFTA02731082, pp.40-41`

- *[alleged]* Wired $1,000,000 in September 2004 to Jean-Luc Brunel's offshore account to found MC2 Model Management, used as a recruitment front — `EFTA01480954; EFTA01657299 (FBI interview of Sergio Cordero, 2010-06-10)`

- *[court-found]* Redirected his aircraft to the U.S. Virgin Islands to prevent FBI service of a target letter on Nadia Marcinkova, then pressured her not to cooperate with the grand jury — `EFTA00081180, plea proffer / Edwards v. Epstein undisputed facts`

- *[court-found]* Threatened and harassed Sarah Kellen against cooperating against him — `EFTA00081180 ¶20`

- *[alleged]* Allegedly purchased Nadia Marcinkova from her family in Slovakia at approximately age 14 and brought her to the United States — `EFTA01186070; EFTA00081180`

- *[alleged]* Directed a $250,000 payment to an assistant in December 2018, days after the Miami Herald series, executed through accountant Richard Kahn — `EFTA02731082, pp.51-52`

- *[alleged]* 'Virtually all' of his wealth derived from money stolen from Leslie Wexner; 'several hundred million' stolen — `EFTA02731082, pp.64-65`

- *[alleged]* Devised a GRAT/mutual-release scheme for Leon Black keeping $2B+ in Apollo assets out of Black's taxable estate; was not a CPA or licensed tax attorney — `EFTA02731023, Senate Finance Committee letter, 2023-07-24`


**Their response:** Denied the federal charges and pleaded not guilty in July 2019; died before trial. His defense maintained the 2007 NPA barred federal prosecution.


**Timeline**


- **2004-09** — Wires $1M to found MC2 Model Management with Brunel (EFTA01480954)

- **2005** — Directs destruction of computers and directories during Florida investigation (EFTA02731082)

- **2007-05** — AUSA Villafaña completes 82-page prosecution memo and 53-page sealed indictment (EFTA01186070)

- **2007-08-21** — Diverts aircraft to block service of Marcinkova target letter (EFTA00081180)

- **2007-09-24** — Federal non-prosecution agreement signed (EFTA01186086)

- **2008-06-30** — Florida state guilty plea activates the NPA; federal case terminated

- **2012-2017** — Receives $158M from Leon Black for 'tax and estate planning services' with no written agreement (EFTA02731023)

- **2019-07-06** — Arrested at Teterboro Airport; SDNY indictment, 19 Cr. 490 (RMB)

- **2019-08-08** — Executes 2019 Trust restated as 'The 1953 Trust' and his will at MCC (EFTA01266204; EFTA00027979)

- **2019-08-10** — Found dead in his MCC cell; ruled suicide

#### Alan Dershowitz  `T1J`

**Branch:** legal-protection · **Roles:** client, attorney, academic, coconspirator  
**CLIENT / ALLEGED ABUSER**

**Knowledge:** actively-involved — Documented conduct as NPA defense negotiator: delivering victims' MySpace profiles to prosecutors, running background checks on the police chief, and an unscheduled visit to the USAO during negotiations. This establishes active involvement in the protection apparatus, not knowledge of abuse.

**Criminal status:** never charged

**Civil:** Giuffre's sexual-abuse claim and his defamation counterclaim were mutually dropped/settled in November 2022. No finding of liability.

**Fallout:** None traceable to Epstein. Retired as Harvard Law professor emeritus in 2013, before the allegations.

**Status, July 2026:** Emeritus professor, author and commentator as of July 2026. No professional fallout traceable to Epstein.

- *[denied]* 'Person named in authenticated victim testimony as a participant in the abuse the NPA was designed to shield' — `EFTA02731465; EFTA01656198`

- *[withdrawn]* Sexual abuse allegation by Virginia Giuffre; her claim and his defamation counterclaim were mutually dropped in November 2022, with Giuffre stating she 'may have made a mistake' in identifying him — `Giuffre v. Dershowitz (SDNY), settled/withdrawn Nov 2022`

- *[admitted]* Led the eight-lawyer defense team that negotiated the 2007 NPA, including the blanket immunity provision covering unnamed co-conspirators — `2007 Non-Prosecution Agreement; EFTA01657816`

- *[alleged]* Delivered teenage victims' MySpace profiles to prosecutors to discredit them — `EFTA01688596`

- *[alleged]* Ran background checks on the Palm Beach police chief — `EFTA01688916`

- *[alleged]* Made an unscheduled 'drop-in' visit to the U.S. Attorney's Office during NPA negotiations — `EFTA01657816`

- *[alleged]* Received $100,487 from Epstein accounts (Jan 2015, Mar 2016), including $85,000 to 'Alan Dershowitz Consulting LLC'; Epstein separately paid $37,036 to Coffey Burlington for his legal representation, categorized by Deutsche Bank among co-conspirator legal expenses — `EFTA01681865 Exhibits P and M`

- *[alleged]* Listed on the FBI PROMINENT NAMES list — `EFTA01648951`

> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** ⛔ JOURNAL ATTRIBUTION UNSUBSTANTIATED. This person is carried in the project database with source documents EFTA02731420/02731465, but that record contains NO page-level citation and NO quoted journal text naming them. The journal sourcing is asserted, not shown. Separately: the DS12 journals are NOT Virginia Giuffre's — they belong to the Doe v. Black plaintiff (1:23-cv-06418), who was also the JPMorgan claimant before Judge Rakoff (1:22-cv-10019). The database misattributed them to Giuffre throughout, which allowed her separate deposition testimony to be counted as journal corroboration. Any independent evidence for this person (flight logs, black book, sworn testimony) stands on its own and is recorded separately — but the journal claim does not support a Tier 1 classification. AUTHENTICATION: court-appointed examiner Gerald LaPorte could NOT date the journal entries — pencil, aqueous gel/rollerball ink and marker have no reliable dating methodology. "No evidence of recent fabrication" rules out recent wholesale manufacture of the physical books; it authenticates nothing. Judge Rakoff denied the claim 31 Jul 2024. Judge Clarke ordered third-party names in these journals redacted going forward.


> 🚩 **Unverified — do not publish without re-checking:**

> - No subpoena enforcement outcome verified as of 2026-07-31.

> - Sharon Churcher, a Mail on Sunday reporter, suggested including Dershowitz in a victim's account ('We all suspect Alan is a pedo', EFTA02731082 p.59); SDNY used this in a credibility assessment weighing against that victim's allegation. This materially affects how the Giuffre-line allegation should be read.

*Also: Prince Andrew, Andrew Albert Christian Edward, Duke of York (former)*

**CLIENT / ALLEGED ABUSER**

**Fallout:** Stripped of remaining royal titles and styles October 2025; ceased being a working royal in 2019; vacated Royal Lodge.

**Status, July 2026:** Private citizen under active police investigation as of July 2026. No public role. Stripped of remaining royal titles and styles in October 2025; ceased being a working royal in 2019; vacated Royal Lodge.

> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** PUBLICATION HOLD — the evidentiary chain behind this classification does not hold. (1) SAME JOURNALS: Judge Jessica G. L. Clarke's opinion of 23 Apr 2026 in Jane Doe v. Leon Black, 1:23-cv-06418 (JGLC) (S.D.N.Y.), states the sonograms she found falsified are in 'the journals Plaintiff used and referenced in this action and in the JPMC Action,' and that the JPMC court-appointed expert and Black's expert 'both examined the journals.' The DS12 journals are the ones the corpus says Judge Rakoff ordered examined in the JPMorgan proceeding. Same physical journals, same woman. (2) THE 'FORENSIC AUTHENTICATION' NEVER ESTABLISHED AUTHENTICITY: court-appointed examiner Gerald M. LaPorte could NOT date the entries — pencil, gel/rollerball ink and marker have no reliable dating methodology. He found no evidence of recent purchase or wholesale fabrication, which is an absence of evidence, not an authentication. Judge Rakoff: 'the controversy cannot be resolved through... laboratory analysis.' Rakoff DENIED the claim on 31 Jul 2024; a stipulation mooted the reasoned opinion, so no particularised findings exist. (3) WHAT CLARKE ACTUALLY FOUND: three sonogram photographs physically altered, traced to a 2006 pregnancy rather than 2002-2005. She precluded Journals 2-4 for containing them. She expressly declined to go further — 'the Court cannot conclude that the journals are wholesale fabrications' — and made NO finding about any handwritten entry. Journal 1, which the amended complaint quotes for the assault itself, was NOT precluded. (4) THE RULING NAMES NO ONE ELSE: Clarke's opinion names no men besides Black, Epstein and Maxwell, and she ORDERED third-party names redacted going forward. (5) NOBODY IN THIS INVESTIGATION HAS READ THE JOURNALS. Every name attribution cites only the bare pair EFTA02731420 / EFTA02731465 — no page, no folio, no date — and the corpus never quotes one word of journal text. The names were decoded by Jeanne Christensen, whom Clarke sanctioned for lying repeatedly to the court and directing destruction of evidence. The LaPorte report is not in the corpus and has not been read. CONCLUSION: this is not a contested Tier 1 classification. It is an unread allegation relayed through a sanctioned intermediary from a journal set a court could not date. Do not publish this name on this basis.


> 🚩 **Unverified — do not publish without re-checking:**

> - Britannica's 2026 timeline places the arrest on Feb 18; CBS, NBC and CNN all place it on Feb 19 (his 66th birthday). Use Feb 19, 2026.

- *[alleged]* Name appeared roughly 300 times in the 2026 releases; a 2003 handwritten letter described the friendship as 'a blessing'; documents show continued contact after Epstein's 2008 conviction — `Jan 30, 2026 DOJ EFTA release, reported by NBC News / Britannica`

> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** PUBLICATION HOLD — the evidentiary chain behind this classification does not hold. (1) SAME JOURNALS: Judge Jessica G. L. Clarke's opinion of 23 Apr 2026 in Jane Doe v. Leon Black, 1:23-cv-06418 (JGLC) (S.D.N.Y.), states the sonograms she found falsified are in 'the journals Plaintiff used and referenced in this action and in the JPMC Action,' and that the JPMC court-appointed expert and Black's expert 'both examined the journals.' The DS12 journals are the ones the corpus says Judge Rakoff ordered examined in the JPMorgan proceeding. Same physical journals, same woman. (2) THE 'FORENSIC AUTHENTICATION' NEVER ESTABLISHED AUTHENTICITY: court-appointed examiner Gerald M. LaPorte could NOT date the entries — pencil, gel/rollerball ink and marker have no reliable dating methodology. He found no evidence of recent purchase or wholesale fabrication, which is an absence of evidence, not an authentication. Judge Rakoff: 'the controversy cannot be resolved through... laboratory analysis.' Rakoff DENIED the claim on 31 Jul 2024; a stipulation mooted the reasoned opinion, so no particularised findings exist. (3) WHAT CLARKE ACTUALLY FOUND: three sonogram photographs physically altered, traced to a 2006 pregnancy rather than 2002-2005. She precluded Journals 2-4 for containing them. She expressly declined to go further — 'the Court cannot conclude that the journals are wholesale fabrications' — and made NO finding about any handwritten entry. Journal 1, which the amended complaint quotes for the assault itself, was NOT precluded. (4) THE RULING NAMES NO ONE ELSE: Clarke's opinion names no men besides Black, Epstein and Maxwell, and she ORDERED third-party names redacted going forward. (5) NOBODY IN THIS INVESTIGATION HAS READ THE JOURNALS. Every name attribution cites only the bare pair EFTA02731420 / EFTA02731465 — no page, no folio, no date — and the corpus never quotes one word of journal text. The names were decoded by Jeanne Christensen, whom Clarke sanctioned for lying repeatedly to the court and directing destruction of evidence. The LaPorte report is not in the corpus and has not been read. CONCLUSION: this is not a contested Tier 1 classification. It is an unread allegation relayed through a sanctioned intermediary from a journal set a court could not date. Do not publish this name on this basis.


**EFTA:** EFTA02731420, EFTA02731465

## Tier 2 — NPA Co-Conspirator (Immunity)

#### Adriana Ross  `T2`

*Also: Adriana Mucinska, Adrian Ross*

**Branch:** recruitment · **Roles:** scheduler, operations, coconspirator, admin  
**OPERATOR**

**Tier basis:** Named by name (as 'Adrian Ross') in the 2007 federal non-prosecution agreement as a potential co-conspirator receiving blanket immunity; never charged.

**Knowledge:** actively-involved — She admitted during a proffer that she was trained by Sarah Kellen to schedule Palm Beach and New York massage appointments for Epstein, and the FBI records that she was instructed to remove and destroy items from Epstein's Palm Beach and Virgin Islands homes during the Palm Beach investigation.

**Criminal status:** never charged. Named as an unindicted co-conspirator with immunity under the 2007 NPA.

**Civil:** Named in victim civil litigation; has invoked the Fifth Amendment in depositions

**Status, July 2026:** UNVERIFIED as of July 2026. Former model from Poland; married and took the surname Ross. No verified 2026 public appearance, testimony or statement.


**Allegations**


- *[admitted]* 'Admits during a proffer she was trained by [Kellen] to schedule [Palm Beach] and [New York] massage appointments for Epstein' — `EFTA01653420 / EFTA01699906, FBI organizational chart`

- *[alleged]* Instructed to remove items from Epstein's Palm Beach and Virgin Islands homes and have them destroyed during the Palm Beach investigation — obstruction across two jurisdictions — `EFTA01699906; EFTA01653420`

- *[alleged]* Listed as a co-conspirator who 'procured minor females to be molested by Epstein' — `EFTA00081180 ¶27, Edwards v. Epstein undisputed facts`

- *[admitted]* Invoked her Fifth Amendment right against self-incrimination when questioned about Prince Andrew during deposition testimony — `NPA co-conspirator analysis — EFTA number not stated in corpus`

- *[admitted]* Immunized as a named co-conspirator under the 2007 NPA — `EFTA01186086`


**Their response:** Has not made substantive public statements; has asserted the Fifth Amendment.

- **2007-09-16** — Named in the defense immunity proposal authored by Jay Lefkowitz (EFTA01186070)

**Branch:** logistics · **Roles:** scheduler, admin, operations, coconspirator  
**OPERATOR**

She scheduled the 'massages' at Epstein's NYC residence, including the visit at which a victim says Staley raped her.

**Criminal status:** never charged. Named as an unindicted co-conspirator with immunity under the 2007 NPA.

**Civil:** Named in victim civil litigation; outcomes unverified

**Status, July 2026:** Private citizen as of July 2026. Interviewed by the House Oversight Committee on June 9, 2026; no charges resulted.

- *[admitted]* Immunized as a named co-conspirator under the 2007 NPA — `EFTA01186086`

- *[admitted]* Invoked the Fifth Amendment through counsel and was never charged — `EFTA02731082, §IV.D`

#### Nadia Marcinkova  `T2`

*Also: Nadia Marcinko, Nada Marcinkova, Global Girl, Gulfstream Girl*

**Branch:** recruitment · **Roles:** coconspirator, operations  
**OPERATOR**

**Tier basis:** Named by name in the 2007 federal non-prosecution agreement as a potential co-conspirator receiving blanket immunity; the FBI was preparing to serve her with a target letter when Epstein diverted his aircraft to prevent it. Never charged.

**Criminal status:** never charged. Named as an unindicted co-conspirator with immunity under the 2007 NPA.

**Civil:** Named in victim civil litigation; has invoked the Fifth Amendment

**Status, July 2026:** UNVERIFIED as of July 2026. Post-Epstein she changed her name to 'Nadia Marcinko,' obtained FAA pilot certification, built the 'Gulfstream Girl'/'Global Girl' aviation brand and founded Aviloop. Reported missing since early January 2024.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* 'Nadia Marcinkova — who participated in the abuse of other victims' — `EFTA01186070 ¶5, CVRA motion`

- *[alleged]* Victims E.W. and A.H. were made to perform sex acts on her in Epstein's presence; she 'participated in the sex acts with minors and Epstein' — `EFTA00081180 ¶¶11, 13, 27`

- *[alleged]* Allegedly purchased from her family in Presov, Slovakia at approximately age 14 and brought to the United States by Epstein — `EFTA01186070; NPA co-conspirator analysis`

- *[alleged]* The FBI was 'preparing to serve Ms. Marcinkova with a target letter when the flight landed in Teterboro' — she was a subject of the criminal investigation — `EFTA00081180`

- *[admitted]* Immunized as a named co-conspirator under the 2007 NPA — `EFTA01186086`


**Their response:** No verified public statement.


**Timeline**

- **2007-09-24** — Named in the NPA and immunized (EFTA01186086)

- **2008-2009** — Visits Epstein 67+ times during his 13-month Palm Beach County jail sentence

- **2024-01** — Reported missing since early January 2024 — no confirmed public sightings


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** DUAL STATUS — record both. The documents describe her as brought to the U.S. by Epstein as a teenager, allegedly purchased from her family in Slovakia at approximately age 14, and some victims describe her as both a victim and a participant. The corpus's own central question: 'Why was Marcinkova treated as a co-conspirator rather than a victim? ... The NPA classified her as a co-conspirator — serving Epstein's interest in silencing her through immunity rather than empowering her through victim status.' This is unresolved. DUAL CLASSIFICATION: also carried at T6 (prosecutorial) in the project tier legend, which places prosecutors at Tier 6 regardless of conduct. Retained at T3 here because the tier reflects the conduct at issue (authorising the 2007 NPA), not the job title.

#### Sarah Kellen  `T2`

*Also: Sarah Kellen Vickers, Sarah Lauren Kellen, Sara Kensington*

**Branch:** recruitment · **Roles:** scheduler, recruiter, operations, coconspirator, admin  
**OPERATOR**

**Tier basis:** Named by name in the 2007 federal non-prosecution agreement as a potential co-conspirator receiving blanket immunity; never charged despite being a named defendant in the drafted 53-page sealed indictment.

**Knowledge:** actively-involved — The FBI organizational chart records that 'at least 10 girls state she is the direct point of contact for scheduling the massage appointments'; she set up the massage table and called victims ahead of Epstein's arrivals; she was the direct supervisor of Adriana Ross, whom she instructed to schedule appointments and destroy evidence.

**Criminal status:** never charged. Named as one of four assistants granted blanket immunity as an unindicted co-conspirator under the 2007 federal non-prosecution agreement.

**Civil:** Named as a defendant in multiple victim civil suits; settlements confidential/unverified

**Status, July 2026:** Private citizen as of July 2026. Operates as an interior designer under 'Sarah Kellen Vickers' / 'SLK Interior Design'. Testified before House Oversight in May 2026.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Named as a defendant in the sealed 53-page federal indictment; Overt Act (2): 'did lead Jane Doe #2, who was then approximately 14 years old, upstairs to Jeffrey Epstein's bedroom'; also took nude photographs of Jane Doe #2 and placed calls to arrange 'massage' appointments — `EFTA01659794 (drafted sealed indictment, never filed)`

- *[alleged]* 'At least 10 girls state she is the direct point of contact for scheduling the massage appointments in West Palm Beach and New York City' — `EFTA01653420 / EFTA01699906, FBI organizational chart`

- *[alleged]* Directed Adriana Ross to schedule massage appointments and destroy evidence during the Palm Beach investigation — `EFTA01653420`

- *[alleged]* 'At least 1 victim states that [Kellen] sexually abused her during a massage with Epstein' — `EFTA01699906`

- *[alleged]* Flew on Epstein's plane with Jes Staley in 2005 during the Florida criminal investigation; had a bank account through Epstein at JPMorgan; 'publicly identified as Epstein's co-conspirators in trafficking offenses' — `EFTA00162121, p.39 (USVI v. JPMorgan complaint)`

- *[admitted]* Immunized as a named co-conspirator: the NPA covers 'any potential co-conspirators of Epstein, including but not limited to Sarah Kellen, Adrian Ross, Lesley Groff, or Nadia Marcinkova' — `EFTA01186086, 2007 NPA`

- *[admitted]* Invoked the Fifth Amendment on all substantive questions about arranging minors at deposition, represented by Bruce Reinhart — `EFTA00729910 p.19; EFTA00798522 p.24`


**Their response:** In her House Oversight interview she testified that Epstein abused her for years, positioning herself as a victim as well as a participant. Her characterization has divided House members, some of whom regard her as a co-conspirator rather than a survivor.


**Timeline**


- **2005** — Flies with Staley on Epstein's plane during the Florida criminal investigation (EFTA00162121 p.39)

- **2007-05** — Named as a defendant in the drafted sealed indictment (EFTA01186070; EFTA01659794)

- **2007-09-24** — Named in the final NPA and immunized (EFTA01186086)

- **2008-01-02** — Becomes a client of Bruce Reinhart the day after he left the U.S. Attorney's Office (EFTA00798522 p.24)

- **2013** — Marries NASCAR driver Brian Vickers

- **2021-12** — Named 80+ times during the Maxwell trial; Judge Analisa Torres called her 'a knowing participant in a criminal conspiracy' but she could not be charged due to NPA immunity

- **2026-05** — Transcribed interview with the House Oversight Committee (reported May 21); Chairman Comer calls it the 'most substantive' testimony yet; transcript publicly released

- **2026-05** — Names three previously unknown alleged abusers connected to Epstein; Comer and Republican members formally ask DOJ to open investigations into them


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** DUAL STATUS — record both. She is an NPA-named co-conspirator with documented operational conduct AND, per her May 2026 House Oversight testimony, a claimed victim: she testified that Epstein abused her for years. The corpus independently records that Epstein 'threatened and harassed Sarah Kellen against cooperating against him' (EFTA00081180 ¶20), which supports the coercion account. House members are divided on whether to treat her as co-conspirator or survivor. Neither characterization has been adjudicated. Separately, a suggested link between her and 'SLK Designs, LLC' (the only entity receiving loan forgiveness in the trust) is expressly marked as speculation in the corpus and must not be treated as established.

#### Donald J. Trump  `T3`

*Also: Donald Trump*

**Branch:** client-abuser · **Roles:** client, politician, executive  
**CLIENT / ALLEGED ABUSER**

**Tier basis:** Sexual-assault allegations from a victim recorded in primary FBI FD-302s, plus documented recruitment-site and social proximity — the Tier 3 standard. No corpus file assigns him Tier 1, and there is no conviction, charge or authenticated-journal naming.

**Knowledge:** unknown — Contested. A victim account (EFTA00019101) describes Epstein elbowing Trump and saying 'This is a good one, right?' with Trump 'smiled and nodded'; a separate FD-302 records only an introduction and that 'TRUMP was polite to [victim].' The FBI's July 2025 memo found 'no evidence that could predicate an investigation against uncharged third parties.'

**Criminal status:** never charged with any Epstein-related crime. No known Epstein-related criminal investigation of him.

**Civil:** Plaintiff, not defendant, in the principal Epstein-adjacent litigation: he sued the Wall Street Journal and Rupert Murdoch for $10 billion over reporting on a 2003 birthday letter/drawing. Suit dismissed April 13, 2026; revised complaint refiled May 28, 2026; pending.

**Fallout:** none. Sitting President.

**Status, July 2026:** President of the United States as of July 2026. Holds the pardon/commutation power over Ghislaine Maxwell's pending clemency application — the central open question of the case. VP JD Vance publicly acknowledged in July 2026 that the administration 'mishandled' the files release.


**Allegations**


- *[denied]* Sexually assaulted a minor victim in a New York or New Jersey high-rise, c.1981-1984 — `EFTA02858481 pp.7-8; EFTA02858491 p.1 (FBI FD-302s, Protect Source 3501.045)`

- *[denied]* At age 14 a victim was introduced to Trump at Mar-a-Lago; Epstein elbowed him saying 'This is a good one, right?' and Trump 'smiled and nodded' — `EFTA00019101`

- *[alleged]* A separate FD-302 victim describes being introduced to Trump by Epstein and Maxwell at age ~20-22; 'TRUMP was polite to [victim]' — this document establishes an introduction and does NOT allege assault — `EFTA00158636 p.4`

- *[alleged]* 2011 Maxwell-Epstein emails strategize about an accuser from Mar-a-Lago and discuss involving Trump himself — `Dataset 9 emails (per Entity Database v2.0, entry #62)`

- *[withdrawn]* Named as a client in an alleged sex trafficking ring at a Rancho Palos Verdes golf course alongside Epstein, with Maxwell as madam — the FBI deemed this complaint NOT CREDIBLE (complainant had 3 police incidents requiring psychiatric evaluations) — `EFTA01660651, NTOC Complainant 3`

- *[alleged]* Jack O'Donnell, COO of Trump Plaza Casino 1987-1990, says he reprimanded Trump and Epstein for bringing underage girls onto the casino floor — `CNN, via Rolling Stone timeline — secondary media, not an EFTA document`

- *[alleged]* Listed first on the FBI PROMINENT NAMES list; the corpus reproduces the list but not the content of his entry — `EFTA01648951`

- *[denied]* A 2003 birthday letter/drawing allegedly sent to Epstein — `Wall Street Journal reporting; subject of Trump v. Dow Jones/Murdoch`


**Their response:** Denies writing the birthday letter; called it a 'dead issue.' Has repeatedly characterized Epstein coverage as a hoax/distraction. He is the plaintiff, not a defendant, in the principal Epstein-adjacent litigation.


**Timeline**

The FBI's July 2025 memo stated: 'We did not uncover evidence that could predicate an investigation against uncharged third parties.' The corpus records statements cutting against the allegations with the same weight as the allegations. Claims of '38,000+ mentions' in the releases come from an aggregator and are UNVERIFIED.

> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** ⛔ JOURNAL ATTRIBUTION UNSUBSTANTIATED. This person is carried in the project database with source documents EFTA02731420/02731465, but that record contains NO page-level citation and NO quoted journal text naming them. The journal sourcing is asserted, not shown. Separately: the DS12 journals are NOT Virginia Giuffre's — they belong to the Doe v. Black plaintiff (1:23-cv-06418), who was also the JPMorgan claimant before Judge Rakoff (1:22-cv-10019). The database misattributed them to Giuffre throughout, which allowed her separate deposition testimony to be counted as journal corroboration. Any independent evidence for this person (flight logs, black book, sworn testimony) stands on its own and is recorded separately — but the journal claim does not support a Tier 1 classification. AUTHENTICATION: court-appointed examiner Gerald LaPorte could NOT date the journal entries — pencil, aqueous gel/rollerball ink and marker have no reliable dating methodology. "No evidence of recent fabrication" rules out recent wholesale manufacture of the physical books; it authenticates nothing. Judge Rakoff denied the claim 31 Jul 2024. Judge Clarke ordered third-party names in these journals redacted going forward.

#### James Edward Staley  `T3`

*Also: Jes Staley, James E. Staley*

**Branch:** finance · **Roles:** banker, client, trustee, coconspirator, executive  
**OPERATOR** · **CLIENT / ALLEGED ABUSER**

**Criminal status:** never charged. Not accused of wrongdoing by law enforcement. Zero criminal charges filed against him in any jurisdiction.

**Fallout:** Resigned as Barclays CEO November 2021 — predates the EFTA releases. Permanently barred from senior UK financial roles.

**Status, July 2026:** No senior financial-services role as of July 2026; banned in the UK. Reported (July 23, 2026) to be set to appear before a U.S. House panel on Epstein.

Was ordered deposed in Epstein victims' suit against Bank of America; BofA settled, scuttling the deposition. [VERIFIED FROM OPINION] Jane Doe v. Leon Black, 1:23-cv-06418 (JGLC): single VGMVPL claim alleging 2002 rape at Epstein's Manhattan townhouse at age 16. Case-terminating sanctions DENIED — the rape claim remains alive. Discovery stayed pending the Second Circuit / N.Y. Court of Appeals timeliness question (Parker v. Alexander). No trial date. Black has NOT been found liable. Wigdor brought three matters against Black in total — Ganieva (2021, dismissed), Pierson (2022, discontinued with prejudice Feb 2024), and this case — not the 10+ suggested elsewhere in the corpus.

**Fallout:** Stepped down as Apollo CEO January 2021 and later as chairman — fallout PREDATES the 2026 EFTA releases. 2026 business consequence: school districts backed out of Lifetouch contracts after Apollo's acquisition.

**Status, July 2026:** Private investor and philanthropist as of July 2026; no operating role at Apollo. Testified voluntarily before House Oversight June 26, 2026 and was then subpoenaed twice by Chairman Comer to return under oath.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Sexually assaulted a victim during a directed massage at Epstein's New York residence, c.2011-2012: she 'attempted to give Black an ordinary massage, but Black began initiating sexual contact, at which point [she] ran out of the room' — `EFTA02731082, SDNY prosecution memo, p.32`

- *[alleged]* 7-8 years of violent sexual assault of Victim 1, with signature violence — biting of genitalia that intensified with the victim's pain — `EFTA02731526; EFTA02731576`

- *[alleged]* Victim 2, independent, assaulted in 2001 at Epstein's Manhattan townhouse: 'he violently bit down on her vulva, labia and clitoris. The biting would get harder and his teeth clamp down more when she yelled out in pain' — `EFTA02731729`

- *[alleged]* Rape of a 16-year-old using adult sex toys, causing bleeding; 'Black and others at Epstein's house would not take her to the doctor and instead flew her out of NY the next day' — `EFTA02731488; EFTA02731662`

- *[alleged]* Epstein and Black referred to the minor victim as 'being 10' — `EFTA02731488`

- *[alleged]* Named among 30+ abusers in handwritten victim journals authenticated by court-ordered forensic examination (gel pen, no evidence of recent fabrication) — `EFTA02731724; EFTA02731420; EFTA02731465; EFTA02731512`

- *[alleged]* Victim text message: 'Leon. You sexually harassed me, sex trafficked me, raped me, and eventually blacklisted me...' — `EFTA02731576`

- *[alleged]* Wire payments to a victim from three accounts — 'Leon J. Black,' 'J. Black Trust,' 'E Trust' — $15K to $167K per wire, running through 2021, across almost 700 pages of bank statements — `EFTA02731526`

- *[alleged]* Paid Epstein $158 million (2012-2017) for 'tax and estate planning services' with no formal services agreement; refused to answer Senate Finance Committee questions on how payments were calculated — `EFTA02731023, Senate Finance Committee letter, 2023-07-24`

- *[alleged]* Witness intimidation: contacted the victim after learning of her statements ('you must appreciate how damaging they would be to me and my reputation'); retained victims'-rights attorney Brad Edwards; victim forced to sign an NDA under duress — `EFTA02731525; EFTA02731577; EFTA02731576`

- *[alleged]* OB-GYN medical records from 2011 and 2019 documenting sexual assault injuries — `EFTA02731662`

- *[alleged]* Securities fraud class action against Apollo Global Management and Black alleging concealment of Epstein-related payments and ties from shareholders — `Filed March 2026 (D&O Diary reporting)`


**Their response:** Says Epstein 'duped' him; expresses regret for the association; denies all allegations of sexual misconduct.


**Timeline**


- **2001** — Victim 2 assaulted at Epstein's Manhattan townhouse; Black gave her $5,000 in cash afterward (EFTA02731729)

- **2006** — GRATs created and funded with Apollo partnership interests (~$585M appraised, expected to exceed $2B) (EFTA02731023)

- **2011-2012** — Sexual assault during a directed massage at Epstein's NYC residence (EFTA02731082 p.32)

- **2012-2017** — $158M paid to Epstein (EFTA02731023)

- **2021-01** — Steps down as Apollo CEO (later as chairman) — fallout predating the EFTA releases

- **2021-04-22** — SDNY AUSA first contacted by Jeanne Christensen about Black (EFTA02731684)

- **2021-05-18** — First and only SDNY victim interview (EFTA02731699)

- **2021-05-28** — SDNY: 'I'm not inclined to open' (EFTA02731578)

- **2022-01-21** — SDNY: 'we are not likely to open another investigation' (EFTA02731593)

- **2023-07-21** — $62.5M USVI settlement becomes public

- **2023-07-22** — SDNY Civil Rights Unit formally declines — 'no evidence of overlap with Maxwell' — the day after the settlement became public (EFTA02731660)

- **2024-03-15** — Victim testifies before Judge Rakoff; forensic examination of journals ordered (EFTA02731724)

- **2026-03** — Securities fraud class action filed against Apollo and Black

- **2026-04-25** — Judge Jessica Clarke sanctions plaintiff's counsel in Doe v. Black; case-terminating sanctions DENIED; case proceeds to trial on the underlying rape allegations

- **2026-06-26** — Testifies voluntarily before House Oversight

- **2026-07** — Chairman Comer issues two subpoenas requiring him to return under oath approximately three weeks later


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** Doe v. Black is the case in which Journal-based evidence is contested: on 2026-04-25 Judge Jessica Clarke (S.D.N.Y., Doe v. Black) found the plaintiff falsified sonogram images in the DS12 victim journals, sanctioned Wigdor LLP and Jeanne Christensen, and barred the falsified entries from trial, while DENYING case-terminating sanctions and allowing the rape claim to proceed. The falsified-journal finding bears directly on journal-sourced evidence against Black. It does not disprove the underlying allegations — the court expressly let the rape claim proceed — but it is a real credibility problem for evidence routed through that litigation. Separately, SDNY's stated ground for declining ('no evidence of overlap with Maxwell') is contradicted by material in its own file, which includes DANY testimony describing Maxwell introducing the minor victim.


> 🚩 **Unverified — do not publish without re-checking:**

#### R. Alexander Acosta  `T3`

*Also: Alex Acosta, Alexander Acosta*

**Branch:** prosecutorial · **Roles:** attorney, politician

**Tier basis:** As U.S. Attorney he authorized the 2007 non-prosecution agreement despite a prosecution memo recommending a 60-count indictment and FBI identification of ~35 underage victims, granting blanket immunity to all named and unnamed co-conspirators. Entity Database v2.0 places him at Tier 3. Never charged.

**Knowledge:** knew-everything — He received AUSA Villafaña's prosecution memo and the drafted indictment and authorized the NPA anyway. Bloomberg reporting (March 2026) established that Villafaña repeatedly pushed her superiors to indict Epstein in 2007 and drafted an indictment — shifting responsibility upward to Acosta and Main Justice.

**Criminal status:** never charged. A 2020 DOJ Office of Professional Responsibility review found he exercised 'poor judgment' but did not find professional misconduct.

**Civil:** No personal liability. The CVRA litigation brought by victims found the 2007 NPA violated the Crime Victims' Rights Act, but the agreement was never voided.

**Fallout:** Resigned as U.S. Secretary of Labor, July 2019 — the original Epstein-driven resignation, predating EFTA by six years.

**Status, July 2026:** Private life / academia as of July 2026. No verified 2026 position.


**Allegations**


- *[admitted]* Authorized the NPA (2007-09-24) despite a prosecution memo recommending a 60-count indictment and FBI identification of ~35 underage victims; the NPA gave blanket immunity to all named AND unnamed co-conspirators — `2007 NPA (EFTA01186086); 2007 prosecution memo (EFTA01186070); DOJ OIG Report Nov 2020`

- *[court-found]* Federal courts later ruled the government violated the Crime Victims' Rights Act in concealing the NPA from victims — `Jane Doe v. United States, 9:08-cv-80736 (S.D. Fla.)`

- *[court-found]* DOJ Office of Professional Responsibility (2020) found he exercised 'poor judgment' but did NOT find professional misconduct — `DOJ OPR review, 2020`

- *[alleged]* Per a lawmaker present, in his September 2025 House Oversight testimony he expressed 'no remorse' for the deal — `Forbes, 2025-09-19`


**Their response:** Has defended the 2007 non-prosecution agreement as the best achievable outcome given the state case.


**Timeline**


- **2007-09-24** — NPA signed under his authority (EFTA01186086)

- **2017** — Nominated by Trump as Secretary of Labor — the same U.S. Attorney who negotiated Epstein's NPA

- **2019-07** — Resigns as U.S. Secretary of Labor after Epstein's arrest — the original Epstein-driven resignation

- **2020-11** — DOJ OPR finds 'poor judgment' but no professional misconduct

- **2025-09-19** — Testifies before House Oversight; a lawmaker present says he expressed 'no remorse'

- **2025-10-17** — His transcript is publicly released; Chairman Comer subsequently asks him to clarify his testimony


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** He appears in the project's Entity Database v2.0 at Tier 3 despite the tier legend placing prosecutors at Tier 6; both classifications are defensible and the conflict is recorded rather than resolved. Bloomberg's March 2026 reporting is materially exculpatory for AUSA A. Marie Villafaña and correspondingly shifts responsibility to Acosta and Main Justice — most older investigation files will need this correction. DUAL CLASSIFICATION: also carried at T6 (prosecutorial) in the project tier legend, which places prosecutors at Tier 6 regardless of conduct. Retained at T3 here because the tier reflects the conduct at issue (authorising the 2007 NPA), not the job title.

> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** THIS TIER SUBSTANTIALLY OVERSTATES THE EVIDENTIARY POSITION. The single source is a complaint the investigating agency itself rejected as not credible, on stated grounds (the complainant had three police incidents requiring psychiatric evaluations). There is no corroboration in any corpus file. **Civil:** none known

- **2019** — Contact with Epstein continues through spring

Tier 4 records documented contact only.

An FBI FD-1023 confidential human source described him as 'a very wealthy Israeli Intelligence (Mossad) Agent'; a DOJ report states that after his 1991 death 'it was revealed that he was working for the Israeli government and the Mossad' — `EFTA01683612; DOJ-OGR-00032040 p.51`

The FD-1023 CHS misdates his death to the 1980s (it was 1991). The corpus's significant observation is about EPSTEIN — that he forwarded an article describing his girlfriend's father's intelligence career — not about Robert Maxwell. Open Question #8, 'Did Robert Maxwell introduce Epstein to Mossad?', is unresolved and unevidenced. Presence in this dataset does not establish criminal conduct. Tier 4 records documented contact only.


> 🚩 **Unverified — do not publish without re-checking:**

> - ALL material about him is hypothesis material, expressly labelled as such by the corpus.

> - One FBI tip ('I suspect Robert, Ghislaine, and Jeffery were all Mossad', EFTA00277076) is an unverified public submission.

> - The DOJ OIG reached the contrary conclusion (EFTA01658887).

**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Juliette: recruited in Cape Town in 2002 at age 20 by an American model who invited her to meet Epstein, calling him 'the King of America'. — `EFTA02731941`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Juliette: Epstein told her he owned a modeling agency that could get her New York representation and that his close friend Les Wexner owned Victoria's Secret. — `EFTA02731941`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Juliette: Lesley Groff called from New York and arranged her U.S. visa, passport and airline tickets; Epstein personally called her mother to assure her of Juliette's safety. — `EFTA02731941`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Juliette: housed in a 66th Street apartment building 'where Epstein housed other models he was abusing', then moved almost immediately to the Virgin Islands where an unnamed co-conspirator instructed her to go to Epstein's room for a massage — 'rape begins here'; abused at the New York mansion, Florida home, Paris apartment and Virgin Islands from 2002 to 2004. — `EFTA02731941`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Juliette: in 2004 at Zorro Ranch, Epstein abused her and took her to meet Bill Richardson, and wanted her to board his plane to California to 'serve drinks to scientist friends'. — `EFTA02731941`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Juliette: Epstein emailed her in 2016 asking if she knew another named woman (also a victim), and in June 2019, two months before his death, emailed asking her to send nude photographs. — `EFTA02731941`


**Their response:** The Epstein Estate, through Indyke and Kahn, asserted an 'Epstein is innocent' defence posture, requiring Boies Schiller to prove the underlying abuse.

- *[alleged]* Recorded phone call in the Palm Beach investigation in which she said 'The more you do, the more you get paid' and offered to schedule a girl and her sister. Palm Beach police originally treated her as a recruiter. — `Palm Beach PD recorded call; 2007 prosecution memo`


**Their response:** n/a. Her own public statement: 'The guilt will never go away.'


**Timeline**


- **2002** — Recruited at age 16; gave one massage; began recruiting other girls at $200 per referral.

- **2005-2006** — Palm Beach PD investigation records her recorded call; she is initially treated as a recruiter rather than a victim.

- **2026** — Public advocacy for full EFTA release; letter to Congress urging release, amplified by Rep. Ro Khanna's office; Khanna's State of the Union guest.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** DUAL STATUS. Robson was recruited at 16 and then paid to recruit others. The corpus and verified reporting treat coerced recruitment as itself a product of her trafficking. Her dual status has been weaponized: a Fox News piece framed her as having 'recruited over 20 underage girls' without the coercion context. She is a Tier 5 victim; is_operator is set true only to record the documented recruitment function, not to impute culpability.

#### Victim 3501.045 (FBI 'Protect Source'; referred to throughout the FD-302s as 'II')  `T5`

*Also: Protect Source 3501.045*

**Branch:** victim · **Roles:** victim, trafficked, witness, recruiter

**Tier basis:** Tier 5 stated by the corpus, with is_public=false; identified only by FBI file identifier.

**Knowledge:** n/a — n/a — her recruitment activity is recorded by the corpus as survival behaviour under direct threat, not culpability.

**Criminal status:** never charged

**Fallout:** n/a

**Status, July 2026:** Unknown; no justice. She was never a complainant in any charged case and no prosecution resulted from her account. Living in Washington State as of 2019. Identity protected (is_public=false).


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY this victim: abused by Jeffrey Epstein beginning at approximately age 13 on Hilton Head Island, SC; drugged with cocaine, alcohol and marijuana simultaneously; forced oral sex. — `EFTA01245620 p.3; EFTA02858481; EFTA02858491`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY this victim: sexually assaulted by Donald Trump at age 13-15 in 'a very tall building with huge rooms' in New York or New Jersey — forced oral sex; she bit him; he 'pulled her hair and punched her on the side of her head.' — `EFTA02858481 pp.7-8; EFTA02858491 p.1`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY this victim: sexually assaulted by 'Jim Atkins (phonetic)' on more than one occasion beginning at approximately age 14-15. — `EFTA02858481 p.2`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY this victim: her mother was blackmailed by Epstein and Atkins using explicit photographs of her, resulting in the mother's federal embezzlement conviction. — `EFTA02858481 pp.2-3; EFTA02858491 p.1`

- *[alleged]* Conduct she was coerced into, recorded as victim survival behaviour and not as culpability: she was sent by Epstein on 'missions' to recruit 'young fresh meat girls. Virgins.' — 'better to get him people to party with, than to be partied on.' — `EFTA01245620 pp.7-8; EFTA02858481 p.5`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY this victim: decades of threatening phone calls she attributes to Epstein and associates; nearly run off Interstate 5 in Oregon. — `EFTA02858491 pp.2-3`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY this victim: asked by Epstein to distribute acid on Hilton Head Island; isolated and given alcohol by Epstein at a Savannah concert at ~15, then found alone by police and held in jail overnight. — `EFTA02858481 pp.7-8`


**Their response:** Trump denies all Epstein-related wrongdoing generally and has characterized Epstein coverage as a hoax; no response specific to this account is in the record. Atkins, contacted by the victim years later, said: 'I don't give a shit if you end up in the gutter. Don't ever contact me again. Your mother knows what will happen.'


**Timeline**


- **1979** — First contact at approximately age 13, Sea Pines Plantation, Hilton Head Island, SC, via her mother's babysitting advertisement (EFTA01245620 p.3).

- **1980** — Assaults by 'Jim Atkins' begin; recruitment 'missions'; Polaroid blackmail (EFTA02858481).

- **1981** — Alleged Trump assault (~1981-1984); two further Trump interactions she declined to describe.

- **1982** — Savannah concert incident (dated by concert archives to Oct 8, 1981 or Jul 8, 1982).

- **1983** — Her mother convicted of federal embezzlement; serves ~2 years in Columbia, SC (~1983-1986).

- **2019-07-10** — Calls the FBI hotline after Epstein's arrest; telephone pre-interview (EFTA01245635).

- **2019-07-24** — Interview #1 (EFTA01245620, Serial 216).

- **2019-08-07** — Interview #2 — names Trump (EFTA02858481, Serial 252).

- **2019-08-20** — Interview #3 — clarifies the assault, describes threatening calls (EFTA02858491, Serial 264).

- **2019-10-16** — Interview #4, scheduled specifically to document the Trump allegations: she asks 'what's the point?', cites the statute of limitations and declines to proceed (EFTA02858495).

- **2019-11-13** — Retains civil counsel; routes all FBI contact through her attorney (EFTA01245633).


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** She did NOT recant — she declined to add further detail, citing the statute of limitations. Her two additional Trump interactions are permanently unrecorded in the federal investigative file. The corpus does not define what the FBI's 'Protect Source' designation formally entails. Nothing identifying is recorded here; her yearbook school year and surname at publication are redacted in the underlying documents. The 'Jim Atkins' name is the FBI's phonetic rendering and the spelling is unverified; a surname match with a journal partial identification exists but the corpus draws no link and none should be inferred.


**EFTA:** EFTA01245620, EFTA02858481, EFTA02858491, EFTA02858495, EFTA01245635, EFTA01245633, EFTA01245629, EFTA01245630, EFTA01245631, EFTA01245632

#### Virginia Giuffre  `T5`

*Also: Virginia Roberts, Virginia Roberts Giuffre, Jane Doe 102 (litigation identifier used in the corpus)*

**Branch:** victim · **Roles:** victim, trafficked, witness

**Tier basis:** Identified victim and central witness who publicly self-identified in 2011 and testified/deposed in public court proceedings.

**Knowledge:** n/a — n/a

**Criminal status:** never charged (victim-witness)

**Civil:** Settled Giuffre v. Maxwell (2017, confidential) and Giuffre v. Prince Andrew (Feb 2022, reported ~GBP 12m, no admission of liability). Dershowitz claim mutually dropped Nov 2022.

**Fallout:** n/a

**Status, July 2026:** Deceased. Died April 25, 2025 in Western Australia, ruled suicide, age 41. Her estate and family continue advocacy; memoir remains a major source. Donald Trump Jr. publicly amplified murder conspiracy theories about her death; there is no evidence supporting them and the official ruling remains suicide.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Giuffre: she was recruited by Ghislaine Maxwell at Mar-a-Lago at age 15 while working as a changing-room attendant and told she could earn money as a 'traveling masseuse' for Epstein. — `EFTA01187465 para.13d; EFTA00208310; EFTA00072560`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Giuffre: Maxwell directed her to give erotic massages and engage in sexual activity with Jean-Luc Brunel. — `EFTA00263917`

- *[alleged]* Allegation made BY Giuffre: three sexual encounters with Prince Andrew in 2001 when she was 17; settled civilly in Feb 2022 with no admission of liability. — `Giuffre v. Prince Andrew (SDNY, settled Feb 2022)`

- *[withdrawn]* Allegation made BY Giuffre against Alan Dershowitz: sexual abuse. Mutually dropped/settled Nov 2022, with Giuffre stating she 'may have made a mistake' in identifying him. — `Giuffre v. Dershowitz (settled/withdrawn Nov 2022)`

- *[alleged]* Allegations made BY Giuffre naming Jean-Luc Brunel, George Mitchell, Bill Richardson and Marvin Minsky. Several named men denied them; none was adjudicated. — `Unsealed Giuffre v. Maxwell filings; 2015 deposition`


**Their response:** n/a (she is the accuser). Several men she named denied her allegations; she partially retracted as to Dershowitz.


**Timeline**


- **1998** — Recruited at Mar-a-Lago at approximately age 15 (corpus dates recruitment ~1998-1999; Entity Database v2.0 states age 16-17).

- **2000-2002** — Period of trafficking described in her accounts; flight records place her with Epstein, Maxwell and Brunel (EFTA01182998).

- **2011-03-01** — Goes public in the Palm Beach Daily News (EFTA00208310).

- **2013-07** — FBI 302 interview, US Consulate Sydney (GIUFFRE001235-1246).

- **2017** — Settles Giuffre v. Maxwell (confidential).

- **2022-02** — Settles Giuffre v. Prince Andrew, reported ~GBP 12m, never officially confirmed, no admission of liability.

- **2022-02-19** — Statement on Brunel's death: 'The suicide of Jean-Luc Brunel, who abused me and countless girls and young women, ends another chapter.' (EFTA00174043)

- **2022-11** — Claim against Dershowitz and his counterclaim mutually dropped.

- **2025-04-25** — Died in Western Australia, age 41; officially ruled suicide.

- **2025-10** — Posthumous memoir 'Nobody's Girl' published.

- **2026-01/02** — Released files contain a Maxwell email apparently confirming the authenticity of the Giuffre/Andrew photograph — posthumous corroboration contradicting Andrew's long-standing denial.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** Her allegation against Dershowitz was partially retracted and mutually dropped; that is recorded alongside her other allegations, none of which was adjudicated on the merits.


**EFTA:** EFTA01187465, EFTA00208310, EFTA00072560, EFTA00263917, EFTA01182998, EFTA00174043

**Sources:** [1](https://www.nbcnews.com/news/us-news/virginia-giuffre-one-jeffrey-epsteins-prominent-abuse-survivors-dies-s-rcna203027) · [2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virginia_Giuffre) · [3](https://verdict.justia.com/2025/11/19/nobodys-girl-tells-of-virginia-roberts-giuffres-life-of-much-abuse-and-very-little-justice) · [4](https://www.cbsnews.com/news/former-prince-andrew-arrested-epstein-files-suspected-misconduct-public-office/)

#### A. Marie Villafana  `T6`

*Also: A. Marie Villafaña, Marie Villafana*

**Branch:** prosecutorial · **Roles:** prosecutor

**Tier basis:** Lead federal prosecutor (AUSA, SDFL) on the 2007-2008 Epstein matter; Tier 6 covers prosecutors.

**Knowledge:** knew-everything — She drafted the 82-page prosecution memorandum and the 53-page sealed indictment and conducted the charging-versus-immunity discussions; she knew the full evidentiary picture.

**Criminal status:** never charged (status_verification.md, July 2026). She received a DOJ OPR finding of poor judgment in the 2020 review; not misconduct.

**Fallout:** Left the U.S. Attorney's Office (SDFL) in 2018; no 2026 fallout.

**Status, July 2026:** Alive. Attorney (previously counsel at the HHS Office of Inspector General); current role unverified.


**Allegations:** none recorded in the corpus against this person.


**Their response:** Has previously apologized to victims for the handling of the case.


**Timeline**


- **2007-05** — Completes an 82-page prosecution memorandum and a 53-page sealed indictment naming 19 Jane Does and Sarah Kellen as a defendant (EFTA01186070; EFTA01659794).

- **2007-09-17** — Asks defence counsel to send documents to her home email address; the CVRA filing notes the government produced no emails from prosecutors' personal accounts in discovery.

- **2007-09-19** — To defence counsel: 'I have had an 82-page pros memo and 53-page indictment sitting on the shelf since May to engage in these negotiations. There has to be an ending date.' (EFTA01186070)

- **2007-09-24** — NPA signed; defence counsel Lefkowitz asks her to 'keep this from becoming public' (EFTA01186086).

- **2008-06** — Still preparing the Operation Leap Year grand jury presentation; grand jury subpoenas issued; her June 2008 travel memo describes the Brunel/MC2 connection and planned New York victims (EFTA00225378).

- **2008-06-30** — Epstein's state guilty plea activates the NPA and terminates the federal case.

- **2018** — Leaves the U.S. Attorney's Office, SDFL.

- **2020** — DOJ OPR review finds poor judgment; no professional misconduct finding.

- **2026-03-20** — Bloomberg reporting, based on newly released documents, establishes that she repeatedly pushed her superiors to indict Epstein in 2007 and drafted an indictment. Follow-up reporting March 25, 2026.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** SHE IS NOT A FAILURE POINT, AND THE RECORD MUST SAY SO PLAINLY. Bloomberg reporting of March 20 and March 25, 2026, based on newly released documents, established that Villafana REPEATEDLY PUSHED HER SUPERIORS TO INDICT EPSTEIN IN 2007 AND DRAFTED THE INDICTMENT — and was overruled. This directly undercuts any framing of her as a willing participant in the sweetheart deal and shifts responsibility upward to Acosta and Main Justice. status_verification.md flags this as a correction most older investigation files will need. Two secondary items remain in the record without adverse inference: the request that defence counsel use her home email address, and an internal email stating 'recusal was connected to reinhart-marie conversation' (EFTA01001371) linking the SDFL office-wide recusal to her conversation with Bruce Reinhart.


**EFTA:** EFTA01186070, EFTA01186086, EFTA01659794, EFTA00225378, EFTA01001371

**Sources:** [1](https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-03-20/prosecutor-pushed-her-bosses-for-epstein-s-arrest-multiple-times-in-2007) · [2](https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-03-25/how-jeffrey-epstein-prosecutor-marie-villafana-pushed-for-an-indictment-in-2007) · [3](https://news.bloomberglaw.com/litigation/prosecutor-pushed-her-bosses-for-epsteins-arrest-multiple-times)

The corpus flags a conflict, not a crime. (2) In 2026 he and Brittany Henderson pushed courts to shut down the DOJ files site because it exposed victims, including minors — the sharpest expression of the 2026 tension in which transparency advocates and victims' counsel ended up partly on opposite sides. #### Bruce E. Reinhart  `T6`

**Branch:** prosecutorial · **Roles:** prosecutor, attorney, judge

**Tier basis:** AUSA who left the Epstein prosecution office and immediately began representing Epstein's named co-conspirators and staff, later appointed U.S. Magistrate Judge in the same district; Tier 6 covers prosecutors, defence attorneys and judges. Specific documented misconduct places him among the accountability figures.

**Knowledge:** knew-everything — The government formally admitted that while an AUSA he learned confidential, non-public information about the Epstein matter and discussed the matter with another AUSA working on it.

**Criminal status:** never charged (status_verification.md, July 2026: 'Never charged with any crime'). No perjury prosecution followed the government's admission that his sworn statement was false.

**Civil:** None known.

**Fallout:** NONE. He was appointed and later reappointed as a U.S. Magistrate Judge.

**Status, July 2026:** Alive. U.S. Magistrate Judge, Southern District of Florida, as of July 2026. Still on the bench; was reappointed. status_verification.md found no verified 2026 Epstein development, investigation, complaint or recusal fight.


**Allegations**


- *[admitted]* While an AUSA he sought lead prosecutor Villafana's counsel on 'strategies for how to handle Epstein's personal assistants — whether they should be charged or if we should seek immunity for them.' — `EFTA00225378 p.114`

- *[admitted]* GOVERNMENT ADMISSION: '(a) The government admits that, while Bruce E. Reinhart was an Assistant U.S. Attorney, he learned confidential, non-public information about the Epstein matter. (b) The government admits that, while Bruce E. Reinhart was an Assistant U.S. Attorney, he discussed the Epstein matter with another Assistant U.S. Attorney working on the Epstein matter.' (July 19, 2013 responses to requests for admission) — `EFTA00068215, DE 213-1 at 9`

- *[admitted]* He had sworn in a declaration in the CVRA case (Jane Doe v. United States, 9:08-cv-80736) that 'I never learned any confidential, non-public information about the Epstein matter' — WHICH THE U.S. ATTORNEY'S OFFICE ADMITTED WAS FALSE. No perjury prosecution followed. — `EFTA00068215`

- *[alleged]* 'He left the U.S. Attorney's Office on Jan. 1, 2008, and went to work representing Epstein's employees on Jan. 2, 2008, court records show.' He represented Sarah Kellen, housekeeper Louella Ruboyo, and pilots Larry Morrison, Larry Visoski, David Rogers, William Hammond and Robert Roxburgh. 'On information and belief, Reinhart's representation of these individuals was paid, directly or indirectly, by Epstein.' — `EFTA00798522 p.24; EFTA00235751 p.21`

- *[alleged]* $84,234.39+ in documented JPMorgan wire transfers from Epstein to 'Bruce E Reinhart, PA' via Gulfstream Business Bank, plus an undisclosed amount on Jan 19, 2010. — `EFTA01483035; EFTA01578729; EFTA01579090; EFTA01483182; EFTA01581347; EFTA01483368; EFTA01483104; EFTA01483112`

- *[alleged]* His private office at 250 S. Australian Avenue, Suite 1400, West Palm Beach was in the same building and on the same floor as Epstein's lead defence counsel Jack Goldberger, and 'right next door to the Florida Science Foundation' — the Epstein-owned entity where Epstein spent his work release. — `EFTA00068215; EFTA00207880; EFTA00209299`

- *[alleged]* DOJ OPR 'and/or other Government entities have collected information about: (a) Bruce Reinhart's possible involvement in the Epstein matter.' No public action was taken. — `EFTA01657803 p.5`


**Their response:** Has not publicly commented on the Epstein-era criticism.


**Timeline**


- **2007** — As AUSA, discusses charging-versus-immunity strategy for Epstein's assistants with Villafana; tells her he is 'best friends' with Jack Goldberger (EFTA00225378 p.114).

- **2007-09-24** — NPA signed — four months before his departure.

- **2008-01-01** — Leaves the U.S. Attorney's Office.

- **2008-01-02** — Begins representing Epstein's employees and named co-conspirators, including Sarah Kellen.

- **2009-06-23** — First of the documented Epstein wire transfers ($25,000); payments run to March 2011, totalling $84,234.39+.

- **2011** — Files the sworn declaration later admitted to be false.

- **2013-07-19** — The U.S. Attorney's Office admits the statement was false (DE 213-1 at 9).

- **2015-12-28** — Paul Cassell's calls begin; four calls through Jan 19, 2016 are never returned.

- **2016-02-17** — Cassell files a formal CVRA complaint; the perjury referral is routed to the Puerto Rico USAO and bounced across four AUSAs, none of whom acted. The complaint is ultimately dismissed by the Justice Department.

- **2018** — Appointed U.S. Magistrate Judge, Southern District of Florida — the same district.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** RECORD THE DOCUMENTED SEQUENCE, NOT AN INFERENCE. (1) As an AUSA he learned confidential non-public information about the Epstein matter and discussed charging-versus-immunity for Epstein's assistants. (2) He left the office on Jan 1, 2008 and began representing those same assistants on Jan 2, 2008, paid directly or indirectly by Epstein. (3) He swore he 'never learned any confidential, non-public information about the Epstein matter'. (4) THE GOVERNMENT ADMITTED THAT STATEMENT WAS FALSE (July 19, 2013). (5) NO PERJURY PROSECUTION FOLLOWED; the referral was routed away, bounced across four AUSAs and dismissed. (6) He was appointed U.S. Magistrate Judge in the same district. He is NOT alleged anywhere in the corpus to have participated in the abuse. The corpus calls this the most completely documented revolving-door failure in the record, every step evidenced by court filings, bank records, sworn testimony or government admissions.


**EFTA:** EFTA00225378, EFTA00068215, EFTA00798522, EFTA00235751, EFTA01483035, EFTA01578729, EFTA01579090, EFTA01483182, EFTA01581347, EFTA01483368, EFTA01483104, EFTA01483112, EFTA01657803, EFTA00207880, EFTA00209299, EFTA01001371, EFTA00729910

#### Darren K. Indyke  `T6`

*Also: Darren Keith Indyke*

**Branch:** legal-protection · **Roles:** attorney, executor, trustee, admin, operations  
**OPERATOR**

**Tier basis:** Epstein's personal attorney, co-executor of the estate and co-trustee; Tier 6 expressly covers estate executors and attorneys.

**Knowledge:** knew-everything — Knew everything about the estate's financial and witness-control architecture — he drafted and gatekept it, held seven simultaneous conflicting roles and was authorized signer on 15+ shell entities. The corpus records no evidence of his participation in, or awareness of, Epstein's criminal sexual conduct.

**Criminal status:** never charged (status_verification.md, July 2026: 'Never charged with any crime')

**Civil:** As co-executor: the Epstein estate settled a lawsuit accusing its advisers of aiding sex trafficking (terms unverified). The estate also administered the Epstein Victims' Compensation Program and a prior $35 million settlement framework.

**Fallout:** None verified beyond reputational.

**Status, July 2026:** Alive. Attorney; still co-executor of the Epstein estate as of July 2026. Deposed by House Oversight March 19, 2026; deposition video publicly released.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Instructed an Epstein assistant not to speak with police: '[Indyke] told [the assistant] that if she ever needed help she should not talk to the police and that she should call him instead.' Listed in the prosecution memo's named-persons table for 'Told assistant not to talk to police; obstruction'. No obstruction charge was ever filed. — `EFTA02731082 pp.41,45`

- *[alleged]* Cash structuring admission recorded in a Suspicious Activity Report: 'Indyke presented the $7,500 check for cashing, and mentioned that he would be cashing the $4,000 check the next business day to avoid all the paperwork and going over his cash limit.' After being advised against withdrawing cash on consecutive days he withdrew $35,000 in a single transaction. Ten cash withdrawals over $10,000 from 2013-2018 totalling $800,000+. — `EFTA01656556; EFTA01681865 Exhibit J`

- *[alleged]* Routed $6.37M+ in co-conspirator legal defence payments and the unexplained $269,159 payment to Noam Chomsky through Darren K. Indyke PLLC. — `EFTA01681865 Exhibits M and P`

- *[alleged]* Accumulated seven simultaneous conflicting roles — personal attorney, trustee, cash beneficiary, loan-forgiveness beneficiary, blanket debt-cancellation beneficiary (extending to his spouse and entity), spouse real-estate beneficiary, nominee holder and amendment gatekeeper. 'There was no external check on any of these roles.' — `EFTA01266380; EFTA01266403; EFTA01266427; EFTA01266204; EFTA00027979`

- *[alleged]* Named with Richard D. Kahn as SOLE JUDGE of what constitutes 'disloyalty' under The 1953 Trust s.2.5(B), with beneficiaries losing all bequests for disloyalty within two years of Epstein's death. SDNY demanded written assurance eleven months after Epstein's death that the clause would not be used to intimidate witnesses. — `EFTA01266204; EFTA00031989`


**Their response:** Testified to the House Oversight Committee that he did not know about the abuse.


**Timeline**


- **2006-12-27** — Named trustee of the Butterfly Trust at settlement, with Kahn.

- **2014-11-18** — Signs as trustee of the Epstein 2014 Trust; receives a $5M bequest plus loan forgiveness.

- **2015-05-01** — Signs the Amendment and Restatement; blanket debt cancellation added for himself, his spouse Michelle Fern Saipher and Harlequin Dane LLC.

- **2015-09** — Signs the First Amendment; $3M directed to his wife for 2 Kean Court, Livingston NJ; amendment power reduced so amendments need delivery to 'no less than one Trustee', making him effectively the sole gatekeeper.

- **2016-07** — Structuring admission to a Deutsche Bank teller; SAR filed (EFTA01656556).

- **2019-01-18** — Named trustee of 'The Jeffrey E. Epstein 2019 Trust'.

- **2019-08-08** — The 2019 Trust restated as The 1953 Trust; he is the largest named beneficiary at $50,000,000 plus debt forgiveness.

- **2019-09-06** — Will admitted to probate; authorized as co-executor.

- **2020-07-26** — SDNY demands assurance that s.2.5(B) will not be used to intimidate witnesses (EFTA00031989).

- **2026-01-07** — House Oversight Democrats force a vote to subpoena Indyke and Kahn (with Wexner).

- **2026-03-19** — Deposed by House Oversight; Ranking Member Garcia issues a statement. Deposition video publicly released.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** OPERATOR, NOT A CLIENT OR ABUSER. The corpus states there is 'no evidence of his participation in or awareness of Epstein's criminal conduct'; the knowledge rating here is confined to the estate's financial and witness-control architecture. Documented minimum financial benefit under the 2014 Trust chain was $8.25M+ plus unquantified debt cancellation and ~$1.25M in trustee compensation; total entanglement across the 1953 Trust is stated to exceed $58M. Redaction anomaly: his name is never redacted across any trust version while seven female beneficiaries in the same bracket are fully redacted. The corpus flags a conditional tier reassessment if evidence emerges that he used his gatekeeper role to facilitate witness management, obstruction or asset concealment.


**EFTA:** EFTA02731082, EFTA01266380, EFTA01266403, EFTA01266427, EFTA01266204, EFTA00027979, EFTA00031989, EFTA01656556, EFTA01681865, EFTA01424842

**Sources:** [1](https://oversightdemocrats.house.gov/news/press-releases/ranking-member-robert-garcia-statement-on-deposition-of-epstein-lawyer-darren-indyke) · [2](https://www.pbs.org/newshour/nation/epsteins-former-attorney-testifies-to-house-committee-says-he-didnt-know-about-the-abuse) · [3](https://www.newsnationnow.com/politics/video-jeffrey-epstein-executors-indyke-kahn/)
