#### Jeanne M. Christensen  `T6`

**Branch:** victim · **Roles:** attorney

**Tier basis:** Wigdor LLP partner and lead counsel for 10+ Leon Black victims; Tier 6 covers attorneys on all sides.

**Knowledge:** knew-everything — As to her clients' accounts — she decoded the victim journals, assembled the bank statements and proffers, and pressed SDNY over three years.

**Criminal status:** never charged (status_verification.md, July 2026: 'Never charged with any crime')

**Civil:** SANCTIONED, April 2026, Judge Jessica Clarke (S.D.N.Y.), Doe v. Black. Findings: she lied repeatedly to the court and to opposing counsel about related litigation, and directed the plaintiff to destroy a social media account; the plaintiff had falsified sonogram images in personal journals. Sanctions imposed: she must file the sanctions opinion in any Second Circuit case she appears in for one year; she and Wigdor LLP must pay the defendant's attorneys' fees; the plaintiff is barred from using the falsified journal entries at trial; and the jury will be instructed about the falsifications and the account deletion. CASE-TERMINATING SANCTIONS WERE DENIED — the court held lesser sanctions sufficed and THE RAPE CLAIM PROCEEDS TO TRIAL.

**Fallout:** The sanctions themselves (April 25, 2026). No disbarment verified. Wigdor LLP sought to withdraw from the Black case.

**Status, July 2026:** Alive. Partner, Wigdor LLP, as of July 2026.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* No allegation of criminal conduct. She presented evidence repeatedly to SDNY and DANY from April 2021 — bank statements, victim proffers, journals, photographs — and 'never received a substantive response.' — `EFTA02731684; EFTA02731526; EFTA02731633; EFTA02731718`

- *[alleged]* On the second Black victim's independent corroboration: 'it is too abnormal to make up... There is nothing publicly out there about the details of his specific sex acts with our client, so there is no way she could know.' — `EFTA02731729`

- *[alleged]* On the settlement: 'Leon Black paid 62.5 million to USVI... it's outrageous that criminal charges have not been brought'; 'one lawyer represents ten women that he sexually assaulted.' — `EFTA02731484`

- *[alleged]* Reported to SDNY 'at least one video circulating on some disgusting sex site' — 6 men present, the victim with 3; 'One of them is a former friend of Leon Black's and both men likely knew JE.' — `EFTA02731515`

- *[court-found]* SANCTIONED. On April 25, 2026 Judge Jessica Clarke (S.D.N.Y.) sanctioned her in Doe v. Black, finding she 'lied repeatedly to the Court and to opposing counsel' about related litigation and directed the plaintiff to destroy a social media account. The court also found the plaintiff had falsified sonogram images in personal journals. — `Doe v. Black (S.D.N.Y.), sanctions opinion Apr 25, 2026`


**Their response:** Unverified — no direct response located. Wigdor LLP separately sought to withdraw from the Black case.


**Timeline**


- **2021-04-22** — First contacts the SDNY AUSA about Leon Black (EFTA02731684).

- **2021-05-27** — Sends bank statements to SDNY (EFTA02731526).

- **2021-08-09** — Reports the second, independent victim (EFTA02731729).

- **2023-05-26** — Reports three victims including a minor (EFTA02731488).

- **2023-07-21** — 'It's outrageous that criminal charges have not been brought' (EFTA02731484).

- **2024-05-30** — Wigdor briefs SDNY on the journal authentication results (EFTA02731724).

- **2024-07-09** — Marks her request 'urgent' (EFTA02731633; EFTA02731718).

- **2026-04-25** — Judge Jessica Clarke sanctions her and Wigdor LLP in Doe v. Black.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** This record must be read whole and neutrally. The sanctions are serious and judicially found. They do NOT disprove the underlying allegation — the court expressly let the rape claim proceed to trial and denied case-terminating sanctions. They are, however, a genuine credibility problem for evidence sourced through that litigation, particularly the journal material; the project's Tier 1 expansion premised on 'forensically authenticated victim journals' needs auditing against this ruling.

#### Richard D. Kahn  `T6`

**Branch:** finance · **Roles:** executor, trustee, admin, operations, banker  
**OPERATOR**

**Tier basis:** Accountant, co-executor of the Epstein estate and co-trustee; Tier 6 expressly covers estate executors.

**Knowledge:** knew-everything — Knew everything about the estate's finances — he ran HBRK Associates, through which all Epstein entities were administered, and executed the payments the prosecution memo examined. The corpus makes no finding on his knowledge of the criminal sexual conduct.

**Criminal status:** never charged (status_verification.md, July 2026: 'Never charged with any crime')

**Civil:** Same estate settlement as Indyke — the estate settled claims that its advisers aided sex trafficking. No personal finding of liability.

**Fallout:** None verified.

**Status, July 2026:** Alive. Accountant; still co-executor of the Epstein estate as of July 2026.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Executed the $250,000 wire to an Epstein assistant in December 2018, days after the Miami Herald 'Perversion of Justice' series, at Epstein's direction. Epstein 'instructed recipients not to tell anyone about the payments.' SDNY probed the payment; its assessment is in the redacted portion of the memo and no charge followed. — `EFTA02731082 pp.51-52`

- *[alleged]* Ran HBRK Associates Inc (575 Lexington Ave, NYC), the management company through which all Epstein entities were administered, and 'regularly corresponded with Jeff Fuller at MC2 about financial reporting, credit lines, and wire transfers' — i.e. managed the finances of the documented trafficking front. — `EFTA01424842; EFTA01870235; EFTA01809528; EFTA01938180`

- *[alleged]* Second-largest named beneficiary of The 1953 Trust ($25,000,000 plus debt forgiveness) while serving as co-executor and co-trustee; named with Indyke as sole judge of 'disloyalty' under s.2.5(B). — `EFTA01266204; EFTA00027979`

- *[alleged]* When Deutsche Bank compliance asked in 2016 what the Butterfly Trust was for, he told them to speak with Stewart Oldfield; the purpose was never documented in the compliance file. A separate RDC compliance alert concerning a Richard Kahn banned from NYSE Arca was cleared as a 'false positive' on the basis of Kahn's own email attesting he was not the same individual. — `EFTA01421789`


**Their response:** Unverified — no direct quote located; his House Oversight deposition video was released.


**Timeline**


- **2006-12-27** — Named trustee of the Butterfly Trust with Indyke.

- **2013-11** — AML Compliance clears the RDC alert on him as a false positive (EFTA01421789).

- **2014-11-18** — $2M bequest in the original 2014 Trust.

- **2015-05-01** — Bequest raised to $5M; blanket debt cancellation added for himself, Lisa Kahn and Coatue Enterprises LLC.

- **2018-12** — Wires $250,000 to an Epstein assistant at Epstein's direction, days after the Miami Herald series.

- **2019-01-18** — Named trustee of the 2019 Trust.

- **2019-09-06** — Authorized as co-executor at probate.

- **2026-01-07** — House Oversight Democrats force a vote to subpoena Indyke and Kahn.

- **2026-03** — Deposed; deposition video publicly released.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** Operator of the financial infrastructure; not alleged to be an abuser. Indyke and Kahn were the only two individuals who received the complete package — cash bequest, loan forgiveness and blanket debt cancellation extending to spouses and entities.


**EFTA:** EFTA02731082, EFTA01266380, EFTA01266403, EFTA01266204, EFTA00027979, EFTA01424842, EFTA01421789, EFTA01681865

#### Todd Blanche  `T6`

**Branch:** institution-officer · **Roles:** prosecutor, oversight

**Tier basis:** Deputy Attorney General overseeing DOJ's EFTA compliance; Tier 6 covers DOJ officials. He is at the centre of the 2026 accountability fight.

**Knowledge:** knew-everything — As the DOJ official overseeing the EFTA production and the DOJ interviews of Ghislaine Maxwell.

**Criminal status:** never charged (status_verification.md, July 2026: 'Never charged with any crime')

**Civil:** None known.

**Fallout:** None to date — but his career is actively at risk. His Attorney General nomination stalled in the Senate Judiciary Committee on July 29-30, 2026.

**Status, July 2026:** Alive. Deputy Attorney General as of July 2026; nominated to be Attorney General after Pam Bondi's April 2, 2026 firing; NOMINATION STALLED, NOT CONFIRMED as of July 31, 2026.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Announced the January 30, 2026 release as the final major one; survivors and Reps. Khanna and Massie accuse DOJ of stonewalling. — `DOJ press releases; congressional statements`

- *[alleged]* Conducted the secretive DOJ interviews of Ghislaine Maxwell; the transcripts were released with redactions that placed him directly in the accountability dispute. — `DOJ; reporting`

- *[denied]* Sen. Ron Wyden accused him on March 18, 2026 of blocking the DEA from releasing the unredacted Operation Chain Reaction target profile, which would reveal the names of 12 co-targets and 2 businesses. Blanche called Wyden's allegation a 'completely fabricated story' and claimed the document is available unredacted in a congressional reading room. — `EFTA00173953; reporting Mar 18, 2026`

- *[alleged]* Named respondent in Judge Emmet Sullivan's unredaction order (June 26 / July 2 / July 30, 2026 deadlines). — `D.D.C. order`


**Their response:** Has defended DOJ's EFTA compliance, maintaining the department has satisfied its obligations; defended Maxwell's prison transfer; defended the redaction posture in his confirmation hearing (~July 15, 2026). Called Wyden's DEA allegation 'a completely fabricated story'.


**Timeline**


- **2026-01-30** — Announces the ~3.5 million page release as the final major one.

- **2026-02-02** — States no additional prosecutions are expected from the EFTA materials.

- **2026-03-18** — Wyden accuses him of blocking the DEA unredacted release; he denies it.

- **2026-04-02** — AG Pam Bondi fired by Trump; Blanche is subsequently nominated as Attorney General.

- **2026-06-26** — Named respondent in Judge Sullivan's unredaction order.

- **2026-07-15** — Confirmation hearing; defends the redaction posture.

- **2026-07-17** — Meets Epstein survivors at Sen. Tillis's insistence. The meeting goes badly: Annie Farmer calls him 'abrasive, condescending, and intentionally noncommittal'; survivors then urge senators to vote no, accusing him of 'gaslighting'.

- **2026-07-29** — Senate Judiciary postpones its vote on his nomination (July 29-30), with Sens. Cornyn and Tillis withholding support. NOT CONFIRMED as of July 31, 2026.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** He is at the centre of the accountability fight, not adjacent to it. The allegations recorded here are about the conduct of the disclosure process, not about the underlying trafficking; no allegation of participation in any Epstein-related offence is made against him anywhere in the record.


**EFTA:** EFTA00173953

**Sources:** [1](https://www.cnbc.com/2026/07/17/epstein-victims-todd-blanche-doj-trump-tillis.html) · [2](https://www.newsnationnow.com/politics/epstein-survivors-todd-blanche-gaslighting/) · [3](https://www.npr.org/2026/07/30/g-s1-136265/senate-blanche-nomination) · [4](https://www.pbs.org/newshour/politics/what-blanche-said-about-trump-epstein-files-during-confirmation-hearing) · [5](https://www.citizen.org/article/todd-blanche-the-epstein-files-a-timeline/)

#### Tova Noel  `T6`

**Branch:** institution-officer · **Roles:** employee, operations

**Tier basis:** MCC Special Housing Unit correctional officer on duty the night of Epstein's death; Tier 6 covers BOP staff.

**Knowledge:** unknown — unknown as to any wider scheme; she admitted not completing the required rounds.

**Criminal status:** charges dropped: charged Nov 2019 (SDNY) with falsifying records and conspiracy; deferred prosecution agreement approved May 2021; charges formally dismissed January 2022. NO CONVICTION. Not incarcerated. (status_verification.md, July 2026)

**Civil:** None known.

**Fallout:** No longer a federal correctional officer.

**Status, July 2026:** Alive. Private citizen as of July 2026. Has testified in the ongoing Epstein investigation.


**Allegations**


- *[alleged]* Charged November 2019 (SDNY) with falsifying records and conspiracy relating to the night of Epstein's death at MCC. — `SDNY 19 Cr. 830`

- *[admitted]* Admitted to a lieutenant that she and Michael Thomas 'did not complete the 3am and 5am rounds'; refused to write a memo, cited trauma and demanded union review. Formal allegations: endangering safety, inattention, failure to follow policy. — `DS9: EFTA00039357`

- *[admitted]* Co-signed falsified 4 PM and 10 PM count slips on August 9, 2019 with two other officers who were never charged. — `EFTA02731790`

- *[admitted]* Told the first supervisor to arrive after the 6:33 AM alarm 'Epstein hung himself' before he could see inside the cell, then 'We did not complete the 3am nor 5am rounds.' — `EFTA02731790`


**Their response:** Admitted falsifying records as part of the deferred prosecution agreement; has said she did not sleep through required rounds as part of any conspiracy to harm Epstein.


**Timeline**


- **2019-08-09** — Signs false 4 PM and 10 PM count slips with Officer-1 and Officer-2.

- **2019-08-10** — Epstein found dead; rounds not completed.

- **2019-11** — Charged in SDNY with falsifying records and conspiracy.

- **2021-05-25** — Deferred prosecution agreement approved by the court: community service, cooperation, no admission of guilt as to the criminal charges.

- **2022-01** — Charges formally dismissed upon completion of the DPA.

- **2026-03-26** — CNN reporting based on the released files sheds new light on what prison officials were doing the night Epstein died. She has testified in the ongoing Epstein investigation.


> ⚠️ **Evidence caveat.** The corpus records that the June 2021 dismissal eliminated any opportunity for the guards to testify under oath about what they observed or were told to do that night — withholding by non-creation of a record rather than by redaction. Two other officers co-signed falsified count slips and were never charged; the ~4 AM supervisor who entered the SHU and left without checking any logs was never identified or charged.


**EFTA:** EFTA00039357, EFTA02731790

**Sources:** [1](https://www.cnbc.com/2021/05/25/judge-approves-deferred-prosecution-deal-for-epstein-jail-guards.html) · [2](https://abcnews.com/US/prosecutors-drop-charges-correctional-officers-connection-jeffrey-epstein/story?id=82018907) · [3](https://www.pbs.org/newshour/politics/who-is-tova-noel-a-former-prison-guard-testifying-in-the-epstein-investigation) · [4](https://www.cnn.com/2026/03/26/politics/epstein-files-prison-officials-night-he-died)
